  <!-- Application Controller Logic -->
  <script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      const editor = document.getElementById('code-editor');
      const lineNumbers = document.getElementById('line-numbers');
      const mainView = document.getElementById('main-pvg-view');

      const btnRun = document.getElementById('btn-run');
      const chkAutoRun = document.getElementById('chk-autorun');
      const btnPlayPause = document.getElementById('btn-playpause');
      const btnResetTime = document.getElementById('btn-reset-time');
      const lblTime = document.getElementById('lbl-time');
      const renderModeSelect = document.getElementById('render-mode-select');
      const presetSelect = document.getElementById('preset-select');
      const btnResetView = document.getElementById('btn-reset-view');
      const btnZoomIn = document.getElementById('btn-zoom-in');
      const btnZoomOut = document.getElementById('btn-zoom-out');
      const btnCenterView = document.getElementById('btn-center-view');
      const lblStatus = document.getElementById('lbl-status');
      const lblLines = document.getElementById('lbl-lines');
      const lblCursorPos = document.getElementById('lbl-cursor-pos');
      const btnExportSvg = document.getElementById('btn-export-svg');
      const dropdownPng = document.getElementById('dropdown-png');

      const modalEmbed = document.getElementById('modal-embed');
      const btnEmbed = document.getElementById('btn-embed');
      const btnModalClose = document.getElementById('btn-modal-close');
      const embedCodeOutput = document.getElementById('embed-code-output');
      const btnCopyEmbed = document.getElementById('btn-copy-embed');
      const embedOptAutoplay = document.getElementById('embed-opt-autoplay');
      const embedOptInteractive = document.getElementById('embed-opt-interactive');
      const embedOptScript = document.getElementById('embed-opt-script');

      // Initialize Presets
      window.PVG.presets.forEach((p, idx) => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = idx;
        opt.textContent = p.name;
        presetSelect.appendChild(opt);
      });

      function loadPreset(idx) {
        editor.value = window.PVG.presets[idx].code;
        updateLineNumbers();
        mainView.code = editor.value;
        mainView.reset();
        mainView.play();
        btnPlayPause.textContent = '⏸ Pause';
      }

      function updateLineNumbers() {
        const lines = editor.value.split('\n');
        lblLines.textContent = `Lines: ${lines.length}`;
        let lineNums = '';
        for (let i = 1; i <= lines.length; i++) {
          lineNums += `${i}\n`;
        }
        lineNumbers.textContent = lineNums;
      }

      function updateCursorInfo() {
        const text = editor.value.substring(0, editor.selectionStart);
        const lines = text.split('\n');
        const line = lines.length;
        const col = lines[lines.length - 1].length + 1;
        lblCursorPos.textContent = `Ln ${line}, Col ${col}`;
      }

      function recompile() {
        mainView.code = editor.value;
      }

      // Sync Viewport Events to Status Bar
      mainView.addEventListener('render', (e) => {
        const { drawList, renderTimeMs } = e.detail;
        lblStatus.className = 'status-pill success';
        lblStatus.textContent = `✓ Rendered ${drawList.items.length} primitives in ${renderTimeMs.toFixed(3)} ms`;
      });

      mainView.addEventListener('timeupdate', (e) => {
        lblTime.textContent = `${e.detail.time.toFixed(2)}s`;
      });

      mainView.addEventListener('error', (e) => {
        lblStatus.className = 'status-pill error';
        lblStatus.textContent = `✗ ${e.detail.error}`;
      });

      // Event Listeners
      editor.addEventListener('input', () => {
        updateLineNumbers();
        if (chkAutoRun.checked) {
          recompile();
        }
      });

      editor.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        lineNumbers.scrollTop = editor.scrollTop;
      });

      editor.addEventListener('keyup', updateCursorInfo);
      editor.addEventListener('click', updateCursorInfo);

      // Support Tab key (2 spaces) & Run shortcuts
      editor.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Tab') {
          e.preventDefault();
          const start = editor.selectionStart;
          const end = editor.selectionEnd;
          editor.value = editor.value.substring(0, start) + '  ' + editor.value.substring(end);
          editor.selectionStart = editor.selectionEnd = start + 2;
          updateLineNumbers();
          if (chkAutoRun.checked) recompile();
        }
        if (e.key === 'F5' || (e.ctrlKey && e.key === 'Enter')) {
          e.preventDefault();
          recompile();
        }
      });

      btnRun.addEventListener('click', recompile);

      btnPlayPause.addEventListener('click', () => {
        mainView.togglePlay();
        btnPlayPause.textContent = mainView.isPlaying ? '⏸ Pause' : '▶ Play';
      });

      btnResetTime.addEventListener('click', () => {
        mainView.reset();
        lblTime.textContent = '0.00s';
      });

      renderModeSelect.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        mainView.renderMode = e.target.value;
      });

      presetSelect.addEventListener('change', (e) => loadPreset(parseInt(e.target.value, 10)));

      btnResetView.addEventListener('click', () => mainView.reset());
      btnCenterView.addEventListener('click', () => mainView.reset());

      btnZoomIn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        mainView._zoom = Math.min(20.0, mainView._zoom * 1.2);
        mainView.renderAt(mainView.time);
      });

      btnZoomOut.addEventListener('click', () => {
        mainView._zoom = Math.max(0.05, mainView._zoom / 1.2);
        mainView.renderAt(mainView.time);
      });

      // Resizer Logic
      const resizer = document.getElementById('resizer');
      const editorPane = document.getElementById('editor-pane');
      let isResizing = false;

      resizer.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
        isResizing = true;
        resizer.classList.add('resizing');
      });

      window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
        if (isResizing) {
          const newWidth = Math.max(280, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 300, e.clientX));
          editorPane.style.width = `${newWidth}px`;
        }
      });

      window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
        if (isResizing) {
          isResizing = false;
          resizer.classList.remove('resizing');
        }
      });

      // SVG Export
      btnExportSvg.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const svgData = mainView.exportSvg();
        if (!svgData) return;
        const blob = new Blob([svgData], { type: 'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = 'export.svg';
        a.click();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      });

      // PNG Export Menu
      const btnExportPng = document.getElementById('btn-export-png');
      btnExportPng.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        dropdownPng.classList.toggle('open');
      });

      window.addEventListener('click', () => {
        dropdownPng.classList.remove('open');
      });

      document.querySelectorAll('#dropdown-png .dropdown-item').forEach((item) => {
        item.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          const scale = parseInt(item.dataset.scale, 10) || 1;
          const blob = await mainView.toPngBlob(scale);
          if (!blob) return;
          const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          const a = document.createElement('a');
          a.href = url;
          a.download = `export_${scale}x.png`;
          a.click();
          URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        });
      });

      // Embed Modal Logic
      function updateEmbedSnippet() {
        const mode = renderModeSelect.value;
        const autoplay = embedOptAutoplay.checked ? ' autoplay' : '';
        const interactive = embedOptInteractive.checked ? ' interactive' : '';
        const useScript = embedOptScript.checked;

        let code = editor.value;
        let snippet = `<!-- Include PVG Runtime -->\n<script src="pvg.js"><\/script>\n\n<!-- PVG Web Component -->\n`;

        if (useScript) {
          snippet += `<pvg-view render="${mode}"${autoplay}${interactive}>\n  <script type="text/pvg">\n${code.split('\n').map(l => '    ' + l).join('\n')}\n  <\/script>\n</pvg-view>`;
        } else {
          snippet += `<pvg-view render="${mode}"${autoplay}${interactive}>\n${code}\n</pvg-view>`;
        }

        embedCodeOutput.textContent = snippet;
      }

      btnEmbed.addEventListener('click', () => {
        updateEmbedSnippet();
        modalEmbed.classList.add('active');
      });

      btnModalClose.addEventListener('click', () => modalEmbed.classList.remove('active'));
      modalEmbed.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (e.target === modalEmbed) modalEmbed.classList.remove('active');
      });

      embedOptAutoplay.addEventListener('change', updateEmbedSnippet);
      embedOptInteractive.addEventListener('change', updateEmbedSnippet);
      embedOptScript.addEventListener('change', updateEmbedSnippet);

      btnCopyEmbed.addEventListener('click', () => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(embedCodeOutput.textContent).then(() => {
          btnCopyEmbed.textContent = '✓ Copied!';
          setTimeout(() => { btnCopyEmbed.textContent = '📋 Copy Snippet'; }, 2000);
        });
      });

      // Initial Setup
      loadPreset(0);
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
